Conclusion: 候选扩展贡献了主要的受控路由性能提升；开放路径证据验证的是输出契约有效性(output-contract validity)而非医学临床应用中的临床正确性。这表明该方案增强了系统输出的结构化可靠性，但不直接等同于医学正确性的保证。

Abstract: Mixed-format medical visual question answering (VQA) requires stable option selection and machine-readable free-text output. The two formats fail differently: multiple-choice predictions can change with option symbols or positions, while clinically plausible open answers can fail automated evaluation when serialization is malformed. We address both challenges with an answer-text memory, a permutation-stabilized vision--language expert, and a sparse candidate- expanding router. The cyclic schedule follows prior work; our contribution is to make expert top-2 a routable candidate alongside memory and expert top-1. On a 1,403-case retrospective internal analysis, this expansion improves a matched binary router from 88.95% to 91.73% (+2.78 percentage points; 95% CI 1.57--3.99), with 56 rescued errors and 17 regressions. Oracle coverage rises from 90.31% to 96.15%, and the final submitted configuration reaches 92.23% on the same retrospective split. For open questions, strict generation and deterministic guards produce 475/475 schema- valid participant-facing outputs without repair, retry, or hard-gate failure. Visual ablations reveal substantial textual dependence. Candidate expansion supplies the principal controlled routing gain; open-path evidence establishes output-contract validity rather than clinical correctness in medical use or deployment.

</details>


### [67] [Conditional Flow Matching for Cross-Field MRI Harmonisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00960)
*Baris Imre,Aram Salehi,Levente Baljer,Andrew Webb,Marius Staring,Efe Ilicak*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对MRIxFields2026挑战赛Task 3，提出一个统一的跨场强脑MRI翻译模型，该模型可通过条件流匹配路径在任何定向的5种场强和3种对比度之间进行翻译。模型仅使用3对配对受试者，通过三阶段训练（退化桥预训练、跨场微调、对抗细化）学习速度场，仅6.3M参数即可覆盖所有60种场对与对比组合，推理仅需5步，在挑战评估集上达到平均SSIM 0.909，优于回归与扩散基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同一受试者在不同场强下的磁共振图像差异显著，阻碍跨站点数据比较与合并。现有方法难以在极少量配对数据（仅3对）下实现多方向、多对比度的统一翻译，需要一种高效且泛化能力强的解决方案。

Method: 将跨场MRI翻译建模为条件流匹配路径：由于源和目标体积已空间配准，学习一个将源切片直接输送到目标切片的速度场（而非从噪声出发）。统一模型采用三阶段训练：(1) 退化桥预训练，从丰富的无配对回顾队列中蒸馏恢复先验；(2) 跨场微调，在所有定向对上基于配对队列训练；(3) 对抗细化以锐化输出。推理时使用二阶Heun求解器仅需数步即可完成积分。

Result: 无需任何配对数据学习的恢复先验即可达到平均SSIM 0.837，后续每个训练阶段均有提升。单个6.3M参数模型覆盖所有60种场对与对比度组合，每个切片仅需5步推理。在挑战评估集上平均SSIM达0.909（三个对比度平均），在全部三个挑战指标上均优于基于相同网络构建的回归与扩散基线。

Conclusion: 该方法证明，通过退化桥预训练提取无配对先验、结合条件流匹配框架，可在极端少量配对数据的条件下实现统一的多场强、多对比度MRI翻译，达到优异的性能与极低的推理开销，为跨站点MRI数据整合提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Magnetic resonance images of the same subject look markedly different across field strengths, which complicates the comparison and pooling of data across sites. We address cross-field brain-MRI translation for the MRIxFields2026 challenge, and in particular its Task~3: a single model that translates between any directed pair of the five field strengths and across three contrasts. We phrase the problem as a conditional flow matching path: because the source and target volumes are spatially registered, we learn a velocity field that carries the source slice directly to the target slice, rather than starting from noise. To learn this mapping from only three paired subjects, the unified model is trained in three stages: a degradation-bridge pretraining that distills a restoration prior from the abundant unpaired retrospective cohort, a cross-field finetuning over all directed pairs on the paired cohort, and an adversarial refinement that sharpens the output. At inference, we integrate the learned velocity with a second-order Heun solver in a handful of steps. A restoration prior learned without any paired data already reaches a mean SSIM of 0.837, and each subsequent training stage improves on it. A single 6.3M-parameter model thereby covers all 60 field-pair and contrast combinations, with inference in five solver steps per slice. On the challenge evaluation set the model reaches a mean SSIM of 0.909, averaged over the three contrasts, outperforming regression and diffusion baselines built on the identical network on all three challenge metrics.

</details>


### [68] [ReFlowSET: Representation-Aligned Latent Flow Matching for SAR-to-EO Image Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00968)
*Jeonghyeok Do,Seungchul Lee,Munchurl Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ReFlowSET提出了一种条件潜在流匹配框架，通过联合SAR-EO重建审计来选择隐空间编解码器，并从头训练一个更小的条件DiT模型，实现SAR到EO图像翻译的最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SAR-to-EO的潜在扩散方法通常继承预训练于自然图像的固定自编码器，但重建保真度在不同编解码器和模态间差异显著，而编解码器的选择会同时影响SAR条件和EO目标的往返保持，因此构成一个关键但被忽视的设计决策。

Method: ReFlowSET包含三个关键组件：(1) 通过联合SAR-EO重建审计来选择编解码器，而非继承预训练的自然图像编解码器；(2) 在选定的潜在空间中从头训练一个显著更小的条件DiT，采用双流SAR条件输入和联合特征精炼；(3) 训练期间将中间带噪EO特征与冻结视觉基础模型提取的干净目标EO表示进行对齐，提供语义引导，推理时无额外开销。

Result: 在QXS-SAROPT和SAR2Opt数据集上的实验表明，ReFlowSET在多种感知保真度和分布性指标上取得了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: 通过系统性的编解码器审计和轻量级从零训练的条件DiT配合语义对齐，ReFlowSET证明了解耦编解码器选择与生成器设计可以显著提升SAR-to-EO翻译质量，且代码和预训练权重已公开。

Abstract: SAR-to-EO image translation aims to generate electro-optical (EO) imagery from synthetic aperture radar (SAR) observations. Existing latent diffusion approaches typically inherit a predetermined autoencoder, although reconstruction fidelity can vary substantially across codecs and modalities. Because the latent codec affects the round-trip preservation of both SAR conditions and EO targets, codec selection constitutes a fundamental design choice; nevertheless, existing methods largely rely on codecs pretrained on natural images. To remedy this, we introduce ReFlowSET, a conditional latent flow-matching framework that selects its codec through a joint SAR--EO reconstruction audit. Rather than inheriting a heavyweight pretrained generator, ReFlowSET trains a substantially smaller conditional DiT from scratch in the selected latent space, using dual-stream SAR conditioning followed by joint feature refinement. To provide semantic guidance for this from-scratch training, intermediate noisy-EO features are aligned with clean target-EO representations extracted by a frozen vision foundation model. This alignment is used only during training and introduces no additional inference cost. Experiments on QXS-SAROPT and SAR2Opt demonstrate state-of-the-art performance across diverse perceptual fidelity and distributional metrics. Code and pretrained weights are publicly available at https://github.com/KAIST-VICLab/ReFlowSET.

</details>


### [69] [Semi-Supervised Virtual Staining via Morphology Preservation and Histopathological Realism Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00984)
*Baoshun Wang,Weiping Lin,Linwu Wang,Yihuang Hu,Baptiste Magnier,Liansheng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种稳定的半监督虚拟染色框架，利用有限配对数据和大量未配对源图像，通过Hessian矩阵推导的形态保持约束和病理学真实性约束来抑制结构漂移和外观漂移，在H&E到IHC（Ki67、HER2）以及FFPE到H&E染色任务中取得了一致的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有虚拟染色方法严重依赖严格配对且精确配准的训练数据，这类数据在临床实践中难以获取且成本高昂。为减少这种依赖，需要探索如何有效利用大量易得的未配对源图像进行半监督学习。

Method: 提出一个稳定的半监督虚拟染色框架，同时利用有限的配对数据和丰富的未配对源数据。针对未配对图像缺乏监督目标的问题，设计了两个关键组件：(1) Hessian矩阵推导的形态保持约束，从源图像提取结构线索约束生成输出保留组织结构；(2) 病理学真实性约束，引导输出符合目标染色的合理特征，防止形态监督退化为简单的轮廓增强或颜色变换。两者共同抑制结构和外观漂移，稳定半监督染色转换。

Result: 在H&E到IHC（Ki67和HER2）及FFPE到H&E的转换任务上进行了广泛实验，在图像质量、形态保持、鲁棒性和下游诊断性能方面均取得了一致的改进。

Conclusion: 通过结合形态保留与病理学真实性约束，该半监督框架有效利用了未配对数据，在不牺牲染色质量的前提下降低了对严格配对训练数据的依赖，并有助于保留诊断相关的病理信息。

Abstract: Virtual staining aims to computationally generate target-stained histopathological images while reducing the cost and time associated with conventional staining procedures. However, existing methods rely predominantly on strictly paired and accurately registered training data, which are difficult and expensive to obtain in routine practice. To reduce this dependence, we propose a stable semi-supervised virtual staining framework that jointly exploits both limited paired data and abundant unpaired source images. Directly incorporating unpaired images is challenging because their generated results lack corresponding targets for supervision, potentially leading to unrealistic staining, morphological degradation, or even training collapse. To obtain reliable supervision from these images, Hessian-derived morphology preservation extracts structural cues from each source image and constrains the generated output to retain tissue morphology. Histopathological realism constraints further guide the output toward plausible target-stain characteristics, preventing the source-derived structural supervision from degenerating into contour enhancement or simple color transformation. Together, the two components suppress structural and appearance drift, stabilize semi-supervised stain translation, and promote the preservation of diagnostically relevant information. Extensive experiments on H&E-to-IHC translation for Ki67 and HER2, as well as FFPE-to-H&E translation, demonstrate consistent improvements in image quality, morphology preservation, robustness, and downstream diagnostic performance. Code will be available.

</details>


### [70] [EvoGS: Modeling Deformation Evolution for Dynamic Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00994)
*Wei Dong,Shahram Shirani,Jun Chen,Han Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EvoGS提出了一种将3D高斯变形建模为时间演化过程的动态重建框架，通过维护每个高斯的持久变形状态、从历史状态外推未来状态，并用MLP观测进行自适应加权校正，同时引入变形感知的稠密化策略，有效提升了动态场景新视角合成对大运动或突变运动的鲁棒性和重建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于MLP的3DGS动态场景方法通常在每个时间戳独立估计高斯变形，对大幅或突然运动不够鲁棒，缺乏对时间演化和历史信息的利用。

Method: EvoGS包含三个核心组件：(1) 为每个高斯维护持久化变形状态，从历史状态外推未来状态；(2) 使用MLP观测对预测结果进行校正，并通过时间残差记忆和演化统计量（如变形速度、轨迹偏差）自适应加权校正； (3) 提出变形感知稠密化策略，沿校正后的变形方向进行克隆与分裂操作，并利用不确定性感知策略抑制变形历史不稳定高斯的稠密化。

Result: 实验表明EvoGS在多个基准上提升了动态新视角合成质量，并在各项指标上实现了具有竞争力的表现。

Conclusion: EvoGS通过将变形建模为时间演化过程（而非逐时刻独立估计），结合自适应校正与变形感知稠密化，有效提升了对大幅/突变运动的鲁棒性，为动态3DGS重建提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Recent extensions of 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) enable real-time novel view synthesis in dynamic scenes by learning time-conditioned Gaussian deformations. However, existing MLP-based methods typically estimate deformations independently at each timestamp, making them less robust to large or abrupt motions. To address this issue, we propose \textbf{EvoGS}, a 3DGS-based dynamic reconstruction framework that models Gaussian deformation as a temporal evolution process. EvoGS maintains persistent deformation states for each Gaussian, extrapolates future states from historical deformation states, and corrects the predictions with MLP-derived observations. The correction is adaptively weighted using a temporal residual memory and evolution statistics such as deformation velocity and trajectory deviation. To further improve reconstruction quality, EvoGS introduces deformation-aware densification. Clone and split operations are performed along corrected deformation directions, while an uncertainty-aware strategy suppresses densification for Gaussians with unstable deformation histories. Experiments show that EvoGS improves dynamic novel view synthesis quality and achieves competitive performance across benchmarks.

</details>


### [71] [CQF-HMR: Continuous Quaternion Flows for Probabilistic 3D Human Mesh Recovery from a Single Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00995)
*Cuong Le,Bao-Long Tran,Pavlo Melnyk,Tahereh Dehdarirad,Bastian Wandt,Mårten Wadenbäck*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于四元数约束的连续归一化流（Quaternion-constrained Continuous Normalizing Flows）方法，用于从单张2D图像概率性地恢复3D数字人体（SMPL参数），通过条件生成模型估计一组3D姿态假设，解决了单目图像深度信息缺失导致的歧义性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从单张2D图像恢复3D数字人体本质上是不适定问题（ill-posed），因为深度信息已丢失。现有的概率性3D姿态估计方法多数仅关注3D关键点，生成的姿态往往不可信，难以直接应用于下游任务（如动画或数字人体）。SMPL-based方法虽有显式身体先验更可扩展，但由于关节旋转的非加性（non-additive）特性，需要更复杂的生成过程建模。因此需要一种既能处理SMPL旋转参数、又能在概率框架下生成合理3D人体的方法。

Method: 提出一种新颖的概率性3D人体估计方法，采用四元数约束的连续归一化流（quaternion-constrained continuous normalizing flows），并以2D姿态估计作为条件输入。该方法针对SMPL模型中关节旋转的非加性挑战，利用四元数表示来约束归一化流的生成过程，相较于其他旋转表示（如轴角或欧拉角）展现出显著优势。

Result: 在Human3.6M数据集上取得了最先进（state-of-the-art）的结果，特别是在姿态歧义性强的场景下表现突出；在具有挑战性的3DPW和EMDB基准上获得了可与现有方法相比拟的姿态估计精度。

Conclusion: 实验表明四元数流的旋转表示方案在概率性3D人体姿态生成中具有显著优势，验证了该方法能够有效生成多样且合理的3D人体姿态假设，从而更好地服务于下游应用。

Abstract: Recovering 3D digital humans from a single 2D image is an ill-posed computer vision problem due to the loss of depth information. Probabilistic 3D human pose estimation compensates for this by estimating a set of 3D hypotheses from a prior distribution via generative models. However, most prior work focuses only on 3D keypoints, which often leads to implausible poses that are difficult to apply to downstream tasks, e.g. animation or digital humans. SMPL-based methods are more scalable thanks to the explicit body priors, but it requires more complex modeling of the generation process due to the non-additive nature of the joint rotations. In this work, we propose a novel approach for probabilistic 3D humans using quaternion-constrained continuous normalizing flows conditioned on 2D pose estimations. Our proposed quaternion flows show significant advantages over approaches using other rotation representations. Experiments demonstrate state-of-the-art results of our method on Human3.6M, particularly in ambiguous settings, and comparable pose estimation accuracy on challenging 3DPW and EMDB benchmarks.

</details>


### [72] [Does This Moment Justify the Recommendation? Counterfactual Behavior-Grounded Evidence Retrieval for Personalized Video Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00996)
*Xin Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出反事实行为驱动证据检索任务（CBGER），区分"个性化证据发生在何处"与"此类证据是否存在"，并构建了包含5,000对受控事实-反事实对的CBGER-10K数据集，以及解耦段级定位与视频级证据估计的CBGER框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化视频推荐在视频层面预测用户偏好，时间视频定位定位查询相关时刻，但强定位能力并不能证明检索到的时刻是推荐该视频给特定用户的有效证据。现有方法忽视了"证据是否存在"（Whether）与"证据发生在何处"（Where）的区分，缺乏对证据可替换性的反事实评估。

Method: 提出CBGER-10K数据集，包含3,026位用户的5,000对受控事实-反事实样本，每对仅替换焦点行为支持的片段，同时保持用户、时间位置和困难干扰项不变；提出CBGER框架，解耦段级定位与视频级证据估计两个子任务，并通过结构化反事实监督同时学习二者。

Result: CBGER在五个适配的个人化高亮和时间定位基线上达到0.4432 MRR、0.6977 Pair Accuracy和0.6987 Intervention Consistency。与QD-DETR相比，MRR提升不具统计显著性，但Pair Accuracy提升了11.03个点。

Conclusion: 准确的时间定位并不必然意味着可靠的个人化证据存在性，这促使在评估"Where"的同时显式评估"Whether"。

Abstract: Personalized video recommendation predicts user preference at the video level, while temporal video grounding localizes query-relevant moments. However, strong localization does not establish whether the retrieved moment constitutes valid evidence for recommending the video to a particular user. We study counterfactual behavior-grounded evidence retrieval, which separates where personalized evidence occurs from whether such evidence exists and evaluates whether model predictions respond consistently when that evidence is replaced. We introduce CBGER-10K, containing 5,000 controlled factual--counterfactual pairs for 3,026 users, where each pair replaces only the focal behavior-supported segment while preserving the user, temporal position, and hard distractors. We further propose CBGER, a compact framework that decouples segment-level localization from video-level evidence estimation and learns both through structured counterfactual supervision. CBGER achieves $0.4432$ MRR, $0.6977$ Pair Accuracy, and $0.6987$ Intervention Consistency across five adapted personalized-highlight and temporal-grounding baselines. Notably, compared with QD-DETR, its MRR improvement is not statistically significant, while Pair Accuracy improves by $11.03$ points. These results show that accurate temporal localization does not necessarily imply reliable personalized evidence existence, motivating explicit evaluation of Whether alongside Where.

</details>


### [73] [SinkPruner: Sink-Free Visual Token Pruning for Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01004)
*Shiyu Li,Zi-Yuan Hu,Shijia Huang,Yanyang Li,Yiwu Zhong,Liwei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SinkPruner，一个无需训练的视觉token剪枝框架，通过视觉清理器过滤高范数冗余token、文本引导剪枝器保留语义对齐token，在减少89% token的情况下分别保留LLaVA-1.5和Qwen2.5-VL原始性能的96.5%和91.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型在处理长视觉token序列时有巨大计算开销。现有剪枝方法（视觉中心或文本引导策略）忽视高范数离群token，即特征范数异常大的token，导致剪枝决策欠优。这些高范数token在特征和空间维度上高度冗余，却常被错误地当作信息性线索保留。

Method: SinkPruner采用从粗到细的两阶段设计：(1) 视觉清理器（visual sanitizer）过滤高范数冗余token，缓解注意力汇聚（attention sink）和注意力分散问题；(2) 文本引导剪枝器（text-guided pruner）进一步保留与文本查询语义对齐的token。整个框架无需训练。

Result: 在12个图像-语言和4个视频-语言基准上的实验表明SinkPruner的有效性、高效性和泛化性。在89% token缩减下，LLaVA-1.5和Qwen2.5-VL分别保留原始性能的96.5%和91.8%。视觉清理器还能提升现有剪枝方法的性能，表现出良好的可迁移性。

Conclusion: 高范数离群token在视觉token剪枝中具有冗余性且常被错误保留，SinkPruner通过视觉清理与文本引导的两阶段剪枝框架有效解决了这一问题，实现了高效的MLLM推理加速，且框架无需训练、具有良好泛化性和可迁移性。

Abstract: Despite their strong multimodal understanding ability, multimodal large language models (MLLMs) incur substantial computational overhead when processing long visual token sequences. To reduce inference costs, recent studies have explored visual token pruning through vision-centric or text-guided strategies. However, these methods often overlook high-norm outlier tokens, i.e., tokens with abnormally large feature norms, leading to suboptimal pruning decisions. In this work, we show that such high-norm outlier tokens are highly redundant in both feature and spatial dimensions, yet are often mistakenly preserved as informative cues by existing methods.
  Motivated by this observation, we propose SinkPruner, a training-free visual token pruning framework for efficient MLLM inference. SinkPruner follows a coarse-to-fine design with two key modules: a visual sanitizer that filters high-norm redundancies and alleviates attention sink and attention dispersion, and a text-guided pruner that further retains tokens semantically aligned with the text query.
  Extensive experiments on twelve image-language and four video-language benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness, efficiency, and generalizability of our framework. Notably, SinkPruner preserves 96.5% (91.8%) of the original performance of LLaVA-1.5 (Qwen2.5-VL) under an 89% token reduction. Experiments further indicate that our visual sanitizer exhibits promising transferability in enhancing the performance of existing pruning methods. Our code is available at https://github.com/LaVi-Lab/SinkPruner.

</details>


### [74] [Low-Quality Face Recognition using Center Aligned Representations and Local Margin Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01014)
*Vedat Can Dilaver,Benjamin S. Riggan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一个统一框架解决低质量人脸识别问题，包含三个组件：Local Probability Margin (LPM)用于估计样本难度、Nested Attention Module (NAM)作为嵌入自注意力机制的低秩适配器、Quality Gating Protocol (QGP)在测试时通过图像质量估计器调制适配器贡献，使单一模型在整个质量谱系上均能良好工作，兼顾LQ识别性能与HQ泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低质量人脸识别因退化查询图像与低质量注册图像匹配困难以及大规模模型训练数据稀缺而具有挑战性。现有FR模型在高质量图像上表现良好但低信噪比图像上精度大幅下降；在低质量数据上微调HQ预训练模型虽能提升LQ识别但会牺牲HQ泛化能力，该权衡在多数据集多质量等级的现代评估场景中尤为突出。

Method: 提出统一框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) Local Probability Margin (LPM)——直接从模型判别景观中估计每个样本的难度；(2) Nested Attention Module (NAM)——一种新的低秩适配器模块，在选定的Transformer层内嵌入自注意力机制；(3) Quality Gating Protocol (QGP)——使用现成的图像质量估计器在测试时动态调制适配器的贡献，使单一模型无需牺牲HQ性能即可覆盖完整质量谱系。

Result: 在监控场景基准(TinyFace, SurvFace)和标准基准(IJB-B, IJB-C)上进行实验，在识别任务和验证任务上均展现出一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 该统一框架有效解决了LQFR中LQ识别与HQ泛化之间的权衡问题，通过质量门控协议使单一模型能够自适应处理不同质量等级的图像，在多个基准数据集上取得一致增益。

Abstract: Low-quality face recognition (LQFR) remains challenging due to the difficulty of matching degraded query (probe) images against low-quality (LQ) enrollment (gallery) imagery and the scarcity of training data for large-scale models. While recent face recognition (FR) models perform well on high-quality (HQ) imagery, their accuracy drops significantly on LQ images with extremely low signal-to-noise ratio (SNR). Moreover, fine-tuning HQ-pretrained models on LQ data often improves LQ recognition at the expense of HQ generalization. This trade-off becomes more pronounced in modern evaluation settings spanning multiple datasets with varying image quality levels. To address these limitations, we propose a unified framework that combines three main components: (1) Local Probability Margin (LPM), which estimates per-sample difficulty directly from the model's discriminative landscape; (2) Nested Attention Module (NAM), a new low-rank adapter module that embeds a self-attention mechanism within selected transformer layers; and (3) Quality Gating Protocol (QGP), where an off-the-shelf image quality estimator modulates the adapter contribution at test time, enabling a single model to handle the full quality spectrum without sacrificing HQ performance. Experiments on surveillance (TinyFace, SurvFace) and standard (IJB-B, IJB-C) face recognition benchmarks demonstrate consistent gains in both identification and verification. Code and models will be released at github.com/candllq/nam.

</details>


### [75] [Fi-ImageNet-1k: An OOD Benchmark From the Inside of the ImageNet-1k Validation Set](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01027)
*Ruslan Rozumnyi,Matěj Suchánek,Tomáš Vojíř,Klára Janoušková,Jiří Matas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Fi-ImageNet-1k，一个从ImageNet-1k验证集中利用ReImageNet重标注的标注错误构建的OOD检测基准数据集，包含655张来自522个类别的图像。该数据集比现有常用OOD数据集更具挑战性，最好的检测器在FPR@95指标下也超过51%，比NINCO数据集难3.8倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OOD检测基准数据集通常以临时随意（ad hoc）的方式定义或收集，缺乏严格的质量控制。而已标注的ID数据集中自然存在OOD图像（标注错误），这为构建更真实、更高质量的OOD基准提供了机会，但目前尚未被系统利用。

Method: 作者利用ReImageNet重标注项目对ImageNet-1k验证集进行重新标注的结果，提取那些未被分配到任何ImageNet-1k类别的图像。每张图像由专家人类标注员在MLLM、VLM和反向图像搜索提供的证据支持下进行审查，与所有视觉相似的ID类别对比；仅保留那些能够被分配到ImageNet-1k类别空间之外特定类别的图像。

Result: Fi-ImageNet-1k包含655张来自522个类别的图像。在测试中，没有任何分类器与OOD检测器的组合能在95%真阳性率下将假阳性率降低到51%以下。与NINCO相比，在FPR@95指标上，该数据集对最先进的监督式OOD检测方法更具挑战性，难度约为NINCO的3.8倍。

Conclusion: Fi-ImageNet-1k填补了OOD检测基准中真实困难样本缺失的空白，为OOD检测研究提供了一个显著更具挑战性的评估平台，反映了实际应用中OOD检测面临的核心难题。

Abstract: Out-of-distribution (OOD) detection predicts whether a test image belongs to none of the predefined classes. To evaluate this task, benchmarks need images from outside the in-distribution (ID) data; typically, these are defined or collected in an ad hoc fashion. Since no ground truth is perfect, ID-labeled datasets themselves contain a natural source of OOD images. We exploit such annotation errors and present Fi-ImageNet-1k, an OOD dataset built from ImageNet-1k validation images that the recent ReImageNet reannotation effort assigned to no ImageNet-1k class. Each image was examined by expert human annotators supported by evidence from MLLMs, VLMs, and reverse image search, comparing it against all visually similar ID classes. We keep only images that could be assigned a specific class outside the ImageNet-1k label space.
  The resulting Fi-ImageNet-1k, with 655 images from 522 classes, is substantially more challenging than any commonly used OOD dataset. No evaluated combination of classifier and OOD detector achieves a false positive rate below 51% at 95% true positive rate (FPR@95). Compared to the recent NINCO, our dataset is 3.8x more challenging in the FPR@95 metric for state-of-the-art supervised OOD detection methods.

</details>


### [76] [ViTAMINS: An Empirical Study of Training Self-Supervised Vision Transformers with Synthetic Hard Negatives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01041)
*Nikos Giakoumoglou,Andreas Floros,Kleanthis-Marios Papadopoulos,Tania Stathaki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ViTAMINS通过在无监督视觉Transformer预训练中引入合成的硬负样本，提升了表示质量，并在ImageNet、迁移学习、图像检索、拷贝检测及图像/视频分割等多个任务上取得了优于现有方法的表现，甚至在ViT-B规模上超过了V-JEPA的ViT-L。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管生成式和自蒸馏方法在视觉预训练中占据主导地位，但对比学习作为一种更简单的方法是否仍具有未被充分发掘的潜力尚未被深入探讨。现有的对比学习框架通常依赖随机或简单的负样本，限制了表示质量的提升。

Method: ViTAMINS通过对现有对比学习框架进行简单修改，在无监督视觉Transformer预训练中集成了合成的硬负样本（synthetic hard negatives），以促进模型学习更丰富、更具判别性的表示。

Result: ViTAMINS在ImageNet及多种下游任务上超越了竞争方法，且资源效率更高：ViT-B配置超过了V-JEPA的ViT-L。合成的负样本引发了涌现特性，学习到的表示包含图像的显式语义信息，分类性能较基线提升高达+11.3%。

Conclusion: 这项工作表明，简单的对比学习框架通过引入合适的硬负样本，可以成为生成式和自蒸馏方法之外的一个强大且更高效的替代方案，值得重新审视对比学习在视觉预训练中的地位。

Abstract: We introduce ViTAMINS, a method that integrates synthetic hard negatives into unsupervised vision transformer pretraining to improve representation quality. Our approach is thoroughly benchmarked on ImageNet and transfer learning, image retrieval, copy detection, and image, video segmentation tasks. Notably, our proposed negatives give rise to emergent properties, where learned representations contain explicit information about the semantic content of an image and serve as excellent classifiers (up to +11.3% over baselines). ViTAMINS achieves these benefits through simple modifications to existing contrastive frameworks and outperforms competing methods while being more resource efficient, e.g., our ViT-B surpasses V-JEPA with ViT-L. Our findings motivate reconsidering contrastive learning as a simpler yet powerful alternative to dominant generative and self-distillation approaches.

</details>


### [77] [Dyn-3D: Unveiling and Resolving Ego-Motion Ambiguity in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01059)
*Jiayu Ding,Zhuodong Liu,Lei Zhang,Manyu Xiong,Hongbo Jin,Haoran Tang,Hongbo Zhang,Changen Zhu,Wenbo Xing*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TempoVista框架与Kinematic-GSPO算法，通过将度量级物理真值嵌入策略优化，解决VLM在动态3D空间推理中的"运动学坍缩"问题，并引入Dyn-3D基准以反事实3D渲染解耦视觉变化与真实运动学属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM在处理动态3D空间推理时依赖平滑轨迹先验而非真正理解物理运动，导致在大位移场景下空间推理能力严重退化（即Kinematic Collapse）。该失败的根源在于自然视频中存在虚假的视觉-运动相关性，且缺乏显式的物理监督。

Method: 提出Dyn-3D基准，利用反事实3D渲染严格解耦视觉变化与真实运动学属性；提出TempoVista框架及其核心Kinematic-GSPO算法，将度量级物理真值嵌入策略优化过程，显式地将视觉表征锚定在3D空间中，利用相机动态作为几何校准信号。

Result: 实验表明TempoVista框架显著提升了运动估计精度和鲁棒的空间推理能力，验证了相机动态作为有效几何校准信号的可行性。

Conclusion: 通过物理监督与策略优化的结合，可以有效缓解VLM的单目尺度模糊问题，使其运动感知与空间推理能力在实际大位移场景中得到实质性增强。

Abstract: As Vision-Language Models (VLMs) tackle dynamic 3D spatial reasoning, ego-motion perception becomes essential to resolve monocular scale ambiguity. However, current models often overfit to smooth trajectory priors rather than genuinely understanding physical motion. Consequently, their spatial reasoning degrades severely under large displacements, a phenomenon we term Kinematic Collapse. This failure stems from spurious visual-motion correlations in natural videos and a lack of explicit physical supervision. To evaluate this, we introduce Dyn-3D, a benchmark using counterfactual 3D rendering to rigorously decouple visual changes from true kinematic properties. Furthermore, we propose the TempoVista framework, featuring the Kinematic-GSPO algorithm. By embedding metric physical ground truth into policy optimization, TempoVista explicitly grounds visual representations in 3D space. Experiments demonstrate that our approach significantly improves both motion estimation and robust spatial reasoning by utilizing camera dynamics as an effective geometric calibration signal.

</details>


### [78] [IT-TextFusion: Iterative Text-Image Interaction with Text-Guided Residual Refinement for Degradation-Aware Image Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01092)
*Siyang Liu,Peiyi Zhou,Tianle Jin,Rongrong Bian,Zheke Jin,Mengze Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种迭代式文本引导图像融合框架，通过多层文本条件特征交互（包括最深层的跨注意力、多尺度跨门控融合和逐阶段文本条件调制），将全局文本嵌入反复注入分层解码与精炼阶段，实现退化感知的全局语义条件化，同时保留可见光与红外模态的互补信息，在多个基准数据集上提升了信息保持与感知质量指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本引导图像融合方法通常依赖浅层语义-视觉交互和有限注意力机制，难以稳健处理复杂退化，也无法充分挖掘文本引导的潜力，因此需要更深入的语义-视觉交互方案。

Method: 提出迭代式文本引导图像融合框架，包含三部分：最深层的跨注意力机制、多尺度跨门控融合、以及逐阶段（stage-specific）文本条件调制。通过将全局文本嵌入（pooled text embedding）在分层解码器和精炼阶段中反复注入，使文本条件能够作用于层级特征融合与残差精炼过程，实现退化感知的全局语义条件化。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的实验表明，该方法在信息保持和感知质量等多项指标上有所提升；但在部分数据集上表现出指标间的权衡取舍关系。

Conclusion: 迭代注入全局文本嵌入到分层解码与精炼阶段能够有效提升文本引导融合的质量，同时保留可见光与红外模态的互补信息；但不同指标之间可能存在权衡，未来需进一步权衡优化。

Abstract: Text-guided image fusion has recently emerged as an effective paradigm for integrating multi-modal information while enabling flexible and task-oriented fusion control. However, existing text-guided fusion methods often rely on shallow semantic-visual interaction and limited attention mechanisms, which restrict their ability to robustly handle complex degradations and fully exploit textual guidance. In this paper, we propose an iterative text-guided image fusion framework that incorporates text-conditioned feature interaction across multiple fusion and refinement stages. The proposed method integrates deepest-level Cross-Attention, multi-scale Cross-Gate Fusion, and stage-specific text-conditioned modulation, allowing the global text embedding to condition hierarchical feature fusion and residual refinement. By repeatedly injecting the pooled text embedding across hierarchical decoder and refinement stages, the proposed framework provides degradation-aware global semantic conditioning while preserving complementary information from the visible and infrared modalities. Experiments on several benchmark datasets show that the proposed method improves several information-preservation and perceptual-quality metrics, while exhibiting metric-dependent trade-offs on some datasets.

</details>


### [79] [P-PatchDiff: Progressive Patch Diffusion Models for Low-light Image Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01123)
*Ruoyu Guo,Haonan Zhong,Maurice Pagnucco,Yang Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出P-PatchDiff，一种可扩展的渐进式patch扩散框架用于低光照图像增强。它动态调整去噪过程中的patch尺寸，实现从局部到全局的渐进过渡；同时引入Multi-Patch Alignment策略，利用全局亮度代理对不同patch尺度下的特征进行归一化，确保整体亮度一致。实验表明该方法可处理从400×600到4K分辨率的图像，比现有patch扩散模型快80倍且显存占用低于9GB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散模型的低光照图像增强方法存在两个核心问题：(1) 使用固定的小patch（如64×64）无法捕获图像级的亮度上下文，导致亮度和颜色估计不准；(2) 增大感受野虽能改善亮度/颜色估计但计算开销显著增加。此外，低光照图像区域间亮度不均，局部增强后的patch拼接回全图时需保证视觉连贯性。这些问题亟需一种既能感知全局亮度又能保持高效性的解决方案。

Method: P-PatchDiff框架包含两个关键设计：(1) 渐进式patch扩散——在去噪过程中动态调整patch尺寸，实现从局部到全局的渐进视野过渡（local-to-global），在不增加过多计算的前提下获取多尺度信息；(2) Multi-Patch Alignment策略——通过估计的全局亮度代理，对不同patch尺度下的特征进行归一化对齐，确保局部增强patch在全图拼接时亮度连贯。模型不追求像素级重建精度，而是侧重可扩展性和全图亮度一致性。

Result: 实验证明P-PatchDiff能有效增强从400×600到4K分辨率范围内的图像；相比现有patch扩散模型实现80倍的加速；显存使用量低于9GB，具备良好的资源效率。

Conclusion: P-PatchDiff通过渐进式patch尺寸调整和跨尺度特征对齐两个核心机制，在计算效率和图像质量之间取得了有效平衡，为低光照图像增强中的patch扩散方法提供了一种可扩展到4K分辨率的高效新范式。代码已开源。

Abstract: Recent advancements in low-light image enhancement have leveraged diffusion models for their strong ability to generate perceptually realistic, detailed images. Patch diffusion models further offer a promising solution to size-agnostic image restoration while improving efficiency. However, existing methods typically rely on small, fixed patches (e.g., 64$\times$64) that cannot capture image-level brightness context, whereas enlarging the receptive field improves brightness and colour estimation but substantially increases computational cost. Moreover, low-light images often exhibit uneven brightness across regions, making it necessary to ensure that locally enhanced patches remain visually coherent when combined into the full image. To address these limitations, we propose P-PatchDiff, a scalable progressive patch diffusion framework for low-light image enhancement that dynamically adjusts patch size throughout the denoising process, enabling a gradual shift from local to global views. A Multi-Patch Alignment strategy is also introduced to normalise features across varying patch scales using an estimated global brightness proxy. Rather than pursuing pixel-level reconstruction accuracy, P-PatchDiff focuses on scalability and coherent brightness across the whole image, allowing the model to perceive multi-scale information and better enhance regions with varying brightness. We empirically demonstrate that P-PatchDiff effectively enhances images ranging from 400 $\times$ 600 to 4K and is 80$\times$ faster than existing patch diffusion models while using less than 9GB of memory. The code is available at https://github.com/RuoyuGuo/P-PatchDiff.

</details>


### [80] [Different Changes Require Different Reasoning: Change-Type-Specialized Experts for Robust Change Captioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01136)
*Jiyoung Park,InJae Oh,Jung Uk Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MEDIC是一个面向图像变化描述(Change Captioning)的新型框架，通过显式建模变化类别并引入类型专属的记忆专家，动态检索类型相关的视觉模式，实现更精确、类型感知的变化描述生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像变化描述方法忽略了不同变化类型（如颜色变化、物体添加）具有不同的视觉线索和推理过程，缺乏变化类型感知能力，导致生成的描述不够精确。

Method: 提出Multi-Expert Diagnosis for Image Change (MEDIC)框架：(1)显式建模变化类别，引入变化类型感知；(2)设计类型专属的记忆专家，根据输入动态检索类型相关视觉模式；(3)通过软路由(soft routing)将输入分配到不同类型的专家，并为每个变化类别学习专属表示。

Result: 在多个多样化且具有挑战性的基准数据集上，MEDIC一致地优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 类型专属的多专家记忆架构能有效捕捉不同变化类型的独特视觉线索，提升图像变化描述的准确性和类型感知能力，代码已开源。

Abstract: Change captioning is the task of generating natural language descriptions that explain the changes between a pair of images. Although different change types (e.g., color shifts, object additions) exhibit distinct visual cues and require specialized reasoning processes, existing methods often overlook these distinctions. To address this limitation, we propose Multi-Expert Diagnosis for Image Change (MEDIC), a novel framework that introduces change-type awareness by explicitly modeling change categories. MEDIC employs type-specialized memory experts that dynamically retrieve type-relevant visual patterns conditioned on the input. This design enables each expert to capture diverse variations within its change type while focusing on the most informative visual cues. By softly routing inputs across type-specialized experts and learning dedicated representations for each change category, MEDIC generates more precise and type-aware change descriptions. Extensive experiments demonstrate that the proposed MEDIC consistently outperforms existing methods across diverse and challenging datasets. The code is available at \href{https://github.com/VisualAIKHU/MEDIC}{GitHub}.

</details>


### [81] [Revisiting Face Recognition for Monozygotic Twins: The Celeb Twins Test Set](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01141)
*Michael Zang,Haiyu Wu,Mrinal Sharma,Kevin W. Bowyer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Celeb Twins Test Set (CTTS)——一个包含80组名人双胞胎图像配对及皮肤标记/镜像不对称元数据的基准测试集，发现当前深度CNN人脸匹配器虽能达到76%以上的准确率，但并未利用皮肤标记或不对称性特征，并探讨了使用生成式AI工具扩充双胞胎训练数据集的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同卵双胞胎的人脸识别是极具挑战性的课题，已有文献指出皮肤标记和镜像不对称性可能是提高双胞胎识别精度的潜在方向。然而此前缺乏带有这类元数据的测试集来验证这些特征的有效性。

Method: 构建了Celeb Twins Test Set (CTTS)，包含80组名人双胞胎的网络图像配对，并附带区分性皮肤标记和可能镜像不对称性的元数据。测试集按LFW、CALFW、CPLFW、CFP-FP和AgeDB-30等人脸验证测试集的规范组织，用当前深度CNN匹配器进行分类评估。

Result: 当前深度CNN匹配器在CTTS同人/异人图像对分类上可达到76%以上精度，但对模型分析表明当前匹配器并未利用皮肤标记或镜像不对称性特征。

Conclusion: 现有深度CNN匹配器虽在双胞胎验证上取得一定表现，但尚未利用皮肤标记或不对称等可利用的区分线索。文章另外探讨了利用Grok、ChatGPT、Gemini等生成式AI工具创建想象双胞胎图像以增加双胞胎训练数据代表性的可行性。

Abstract: Past literature on face recognition for monozygotic (("identical") twins points to facial marks and mirror asymmetry as possible directions for improved accuracy of twins recognition. The Celeb Twins Test Set (CTTS) contains web-scraped image pairs for 80 sets of celebrity twins. It is the only twins test set with meta-data for twins with distinguishing skin marks and possible mirror asymmetry. CTTS is organized in the manner of face verification test sets such as LFW, CALFW, CPLFW, CFP-FP, and AgeDB-30. Current deep CNN matchers can achieve over 76% accuracy in classifying CTTS same-person / different-person image pairs. We show that current matchers do not make use of skin marks, or asymmetry, and discuss reasons for this. Finally, we discuss the feasibility of using generative AI tools such as Grok, ChatGPT and Gemini to create images of imagined monozygotic twins as a means to increase representation of twins in face recognition training sets.

</details>


### [82] [StainPresetNet: Stain Preset Network for Fast Multi-to-Multi Stain Normalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01146)
*Hongtao Kang,Die Luo,Li Chen,Jing Cai,Junbo Hu,Xiuli Liu,Shenghua Cheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出StainPresetNet，一种结合结构保持与数据集级颜色映射的染色归一化框架，通过预设参考图像引导的像素级归一化实现多方向适应性，在细胞病理和组织病理数据集上取得更优的颜色映射精度，较现有深度学习方法降低90%计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有染色归一化方法分为两类：传统方法依赖单个或有限参考图像的像素级变换，风格灵活但颜色映射提取不准确；深度学习方法虽能实现数据集级准确颜色映射，但存在计算效率低、产生伪影、归一化方向固定需重训练模型等问题。

Method: 提出StainPresetNet框架，结合结构保持与数据集级颜色映射。核心机制为预设参考图像引导的像素级归一化，通过简单替换参考图像即可灵活调整归一化方向，无需模型重训练，兼顾计算效率与颜色映射精度。

Result: 在细胞病理和组织病理数据集上的评估表明，StainPresetNet相比传统方法具有更优的颜色映射精度，能有效提升诊断任务中分类器的泛化能力，并相较现有深度学习方法降低90%的计算开销。

Conclusion: StainPresetNet通过预设引导机制克服了当前基于深度学习的染色归一化方法在方向灵活性上的局限，仅通过参考图像替换即可实现归一化方向调整，在精度、泛化性和效率之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: Stain normalization reduces color variations caused by variations in staining protocols and imaging conditions, thereby enhancing computer-aided diagnostic system performance. Traditional methods derive mapping relationships from individual or limited reference images through pixel-wise transformation, offering style flexibility but suffering from inaccurate color mapping extraction. While existing deep-learning-based approaches achieve accurate dataset-wide color mapping through complex neural networks, they face challenges including computational inefficiency, artifact generation, and fixed normalization directions requiring model retraining for directional changes. To address these limitations, we propose StainPresetNet - a novel framework that combines structural preservation with dataset-level color mapping while maintaining computational efficiency. Our method implements pixel-wise normalization guided by preset reference images, enabling multi-directional adaptability without retraining. Evaluations on cytopathology and histopathology datasets demonstrate that StainPresetNet achieves superior color mapping accuracy compared to conventional methods, effectively improves classifier generalization in diagnostic tasks, and reduces computational overhead by 90\% versus existing deep learning approaches. The proposed preset-guided mechanism facilitates flexible adjustment of normalization directions through simple reference image replacement, overcoming the directional rigidity of current deep-learning-based solutions.

</details>


### [83] [On the Design Fundamentals of Pixel Text Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01147)
*Chaohao Yuan,Ruifeng Yuan,Zhuoxu Huang,Yu Rong,Hong Cheng,Hou Pong Chan,Chenghao Xiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Pixel Linguist II，一个原生分辨率的视觉文本编码器，通过可变图像分辨率与渲染字体大小、自然图文配对、布局感知渲染以及两阶段多语言课程学习四个核心设计原则，在280M训练样本上训练，在英文、跨语言及多语言Visual STS和ViDoRe基准上取得SOTA结果，并在80%视觉token压缩下仍保持鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有像素级文本编码器在处理文本丰富的视觉输入时存在固定分辨率预训练、视觉捷径学习、视觉接地弱以及多语言视觉文本理解不足等根本性缺陷。作者希望探究实现鲁棒视觉文本表征学习所必需的基础设计原则。

Method: 通过系统化的受控消融实验，识别出四个关键组件：(1) 可变图像分辨率和渲染字体大小作为高分辨率文档泛化的空间代理；(2) 自然图文配对对于视觉接地和防止纯文本坍缩不可或缺；(3) 布局感知渲染以预防像素级捷径；(4) 两阶段多语言课程实现有效的跨语言对齐。整合这些原则为可扩展的训练配方，训练Pixel Linguist II原生分辨率视觉编码器，采用即时渲染、统一对比接地和多语言课程。

Result: Pixel Linguist II在英文、跨语言和多语言Visual STS及ViDoRe基准上取得新的SOTA结果，并改善了多模态大语言模型的下游评测表现。在80%视觉token压缩率下仍保持鲁棒性，展现出光学上下文压缩的巨大潜力。

Conclusion: 通过识别和整合四项核心设计原则，像素级视觉文本编码器可以实现对高分辨率文档、多语言场景的鲁棒理解，并支持高效的视觉token压缩，为光学上下文压缩和像素级语言理解提供了可行的训练范式。

Abstract: Text-rich visual inputs require models that can read, retrieve, and compress language directly in pixel space, yet existing pixel-text encoders struggle with fixed resolution pretraining, visual shortcut learning, weak visual grounding, and multilingual visual text understanding. In this work, we investigate the fundamental design principles required for robust visual text representation learning. Through systematic controlled ablations, we identify four critical components: variable image resolutions and rendered font sizes provide spatial proxies for high-resolution document generalization; natural image-text pairs are indispensable for grounding and prevent text-only collapse; layout-aware rendering helps prevent pixel-level shortcuts; and a two-stage multilingual curriculum enables effective cross-lingual alignment. By integrating these principles into a scalable training recipe, we train Pixel Linguist II, a native-resolution vision encoder trained with on-the-fly rendering, unified contrastive grounding, and a multilingual curriculum over 280M training examples. Pixel Linguist II sets new state-of-the-art results on English, cross-lingual, and multilingual Visual STS and ViDoRe, while also enabling better MLLM downstream evaluation. Notably, Pixel Linguist II remains robust under 80\% visual token compression, showing great promise for optical context compression. Our code and resources are available at https://github.com/Pixel-Linguist/Pixel-Linguist-II.

</details>


### [84] [Dotting the Eye: An Intent-Driven Image Retouching Agent for Visual Focus Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01148)
*Chujie Qin,Zilong Zhang,Zewei Chang,Chunle Guo,Ruixing Wang,Tao Hu,Ming-Ming Cheng,Chongyi Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出EyeControl，一个由MLLM驱动的智能体配合基于扩散的修图执行器，能够在弱用户意图（仅需少量点击或粗略笔触）下实现视觉焦点增强的图像修图。其核心思想是将弱用户意图与目标编辑区域及相应的色调调整操作显式关联，通过意图解释、注意力图对齐和操作一致性约束实现全局与局部协调的自然修图，并贡献了ControlArt-Bench评估数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统图像修图仅关注通过颜色调整提升整体视觉质量，但实际应用中修图还需通过引导观看者注意力来突出特定主体或区域。这种面向焦点的修图需要精确协调全局与局部调整来操控感知显著性，同时保持视觉自然度，过程复杂且通常依赖大量专业经验，因此亟需一种能在弱用户意图下自动化实现该任务的方案。

Method: EyeControl由两部分组成：1) MLLM驱动的智能体解释用户弱意图和图像内容，推断视觉焦点并生成结构化的意图引导；2) 基于扩散模型的修图执行器，通过将注意力图与设计的伪意图图（pseudo-intent map）显式对齐，增强对目标区域的响应。此外引入操作一致性约束（operation-consistency constraint）以改善全局与局部调整之间的协调性，使修图结果更自然连贯。同时构建了ControlArt-Bench高质量评估数据集。

Result: 大量实验表明，EyeControl能够生成感知上更具吸引力且意图对齐性更强的修图结果，在视觉焦点增强任务上取得了优异表现。

Conclusion: EyeControl成功实现了在弱用户意图条件下由MLLM驱动的视觉焦点增强修图，验证了将意图解释、注意力对齐与操作一致性约束相结合的框架在全局-局部协调修图中的有效性，为该方向提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Image retouching is commonly formulated as enhancing overall visual quality through color adjustment, but in practice, it also serves to emphasize visual focus by guiding viewers' attention toward a specific subject or region. Achieving such focus-oriented retouching is inherently challenging, as it requires well-coordinated global and local adjustments to manipulate perceptual saliency while maintaining visual naturalness. This intricate process typically demands substantial professional expertise. In this study, we propose EyeControl, a MLLM-driven agent with a diffusion-based retouching executor that enables visual focus enhancement under weak user intent. With only a few clicks or coarse strokes, EyeControl directs visual attention to the intended region, effectively "dotting the eye" of the image. The core idea is to explicitly link the weak user intention with the target editing region and the corresponding tonal adjustment operations during retouching. To achieve this, the system first interprets the intent and image content to infer the visual focus and generate structured intent guidance for the retouching executor. Second, the retouching executor is encouraged to respond more strongly to the target region, explicitly aligning its attention map with a designed pseudo-intent map. We also introduce an operation-consistency constraint to improve coordination between global and local adjustments, achieving more natural and coherent retouching. Additionally, we contribute ControlArt-Bench, a high-quality evaluation dataset for visual focus enhancement. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that EyeControl yields perceptually appealing results with stronger intent alignment. Code can be found in https://github.com/DragonisCV/EyeControl.

</details>


### [85] [Monocular Depth Estimation from a Single Image: Progress and Opportunities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01172)
*Muxin Liu,Xiaoyang Lyu,Yang-Tian Sun,Yi-Hua Huang,Ziyi Yang,Peng Dai,Xiaojuan Qi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于单目深度估计的综述论文，系统梳理了该领域从早期基于学习方法到基础模型时代的演变过程，涵盖问题定义、数据集、代表性方法、基础模型时代的新范式（判别式与生成式）、视频扩展以及真实应用（视觉SLAM、内容生成、机器人感知），并展望了开放挑战与未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目深度估计是计算机视觉中的基础性挑战问题，支撑三维重建、机器人、自动驾驶和增强现实等广泛领域。鉴于该领域经历了从传统学习方法到基础模型范式的重大转型，有必要对这一演变过程进行系统性的梳理与总结，以帮助研究者理解发展脉络、关键贡献及未来趋势。

Method: 首先对问题本身进行定义与建模，区分相对深度与度量深度估计并讨论关键挑战；随后介绍常用问题形式与数据集（室内、室外、合成数据）；在此基础上回顾基础模型时代之前的主要进展并提炼核心方法洞见；进而重点分析基础模型时代的判别式与生成式两类新范式，强调大规模预训练（如DINOv3）和合成数据的关键作用，并通过定量基准和定性示例对比代表性模型；最后讨论视频深度估计的自然扩展、实际应用整合以及开放挑战。

Result: 论文系统性地归纳了单目深度估计在不同发展阶段的代表性方法及其核心贡献，对判别式和生成式基础模型方法进行了对比分析，展示了深度估计在视觉SLAM、内容生成和机器人感知等实际场景中的集成效果，并识别了精读、效率与鲁棒性等方面改进的关键驱动力。

Conclusion: 单目深度估计正全面迈入基础模型时代，大规模预训练与合成数据显著提升了跨域泛化能力；未来仍需解决动态场景、度量尺度恢复、实时性能等开放挑战，并向视频深度估计和多模态集成等方向拓展。

Abstract: Monocular depth estimation has long stood as a fundamental challenge in computer vision, enabling a wide range of applications including 3D reconstruction, robotics, autonomous driving, and augmented reality. This survey traces the field's evolution from early learning-based methods to the emergence of transformative foundation models. We begin by framing the problem, distinguishing between relative and metric depth estimation, and highlighting the key challenges that have shaped a decade of research. We then present common problem formulations and introduce the most widely used datasets, covering indoor, outdoor, and synthetic data. Following this, we review major advances prior to the foundation model era, distilling core insights from influential methods that contributed to improvements in accuracy, efficiency, and robustness. The survey then turns to the recent surge of foundation-model-based approaches, categorizing them into discriminative and generative paradigms and emphasizing the critical roles of large-scale pretraining (e.g., DINOv3) and synthetic data. We compare representative models using both quantitative benchmarks and qualitative examples, and discuss natural extensions to video-based depth estimation. Further, to illustrate real-world impact, we highlight the integration of depth estimation into applications such as visual SLAM, content generation, and robot perception. Finally, we outline open challenges and promising research directions as the field advances further into the era of foundation models.

</details>


### [86] [S$^2$Prune: Spatially Structured Visual Token Pruning for Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01224)
*Yuanyuan Jia,Shunpu Tang,Qianqian Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出S²Prune，一种无需训练的视觉token剪枝方法，通过保持空间覆盖并依据Laplacian变化自适应分配token密度，实现MLLM推理开销降低且保持性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于重要性或冗余度的视觉token剪枝方法会产生跨输入稳定的空间偏差，且并不总是优于简单的Uniform Grid采样，说明广空间覆盖具有重要价值。作者由此出发，希望在剪枝中保持空间覆盖同时适应局部图像结构。

Method: 1) 将图像划分为区域，每个区域至少保留一个token以确保空间覆盖；2) 将剩余token预算依据Laplacian variation分配到各区域，结构更丰富的区域获得更多token；3) 使用第一个decoder block计算的Early Representation Change (ERC)选择每个区域内的代表性token。

Result: 在两种MLLM架构上进行了多场景评估。在Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct上，S²Prune在所评估的训练无需剪枝方法中取得最高平均准确率。仅使用原始576个视觉token中的32个时，仍保留全模型79.3%的性能。

Conclusion: S²Prune通过兼顾空间覆盖和局部结构自适应的token分配，为训练无需的视觉token剪枝提供了新的有效范式，显著降低推理开销的同时保持较高模型性能。

Abstract: Visual token pruning reduces the inference overhead of multimodal large language models (MLLMs) by retaining only a subset of visual tokens. Existing methods usually select tokens based on importance or redundancy. However, we observe that these criteria produce stable spatial biases across inputs and do not always outperform simple Uniform Grid sampling, highlighting the value of broad spatial coverage. Motivated by this, we propose S$^2$Prune, a training-free pruning method that preserves spatial coverage while adapting token density to local image structure. We first divide the image into regions and assign at least one token to each region to preserve coverage. The remaining token budget is then distributed according to Laplacian variation, giving more tokens to regions with richer structure. We then use Early Representation Change (ERC), computed from the first decoder block, to select representative tokens within each region. We evaluate S$^2$Prune across diverse settings and two MLLM architectures. On Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct, it achieves the highest average accuracy among the evaluated training-free pruning methods. With only 32 of the original 576 visual tokens, it still retains 79.3% of the full-model performance. Code is available at https://github.com/yuanyuanjia71-spec/S2Prune.

</details>


### [87] [One Prompt Is Enough: Watermark Laundering Through Foundation Image Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01249)
*Jidong Yang,Qi Li,Wei Zong,Yang-Wai Chow,Willy Susilo,Huaike Yu,Chunpeng Wang,Suo Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文揭示了基础图像模型对不可见水印构成的新威胁：攻击者只需提交带水印图像并附带单一重建提示词，即可获得视觉保真输出但水印无法可靠解码。作者将此形式化为"水印清洗"，并提出了结合比特错误率与视觉/语义保持的联合载荷-保真度评估框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的不可见水印评估仅针对预定义扰动（如压缩、模糊、噪声、裁剪、去噪），但公开的基础图像模型暴露了截然不同的攻击面：通过重建提示即可去除水印。这一威胁模式尚未被系统形式化和评估，作者旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 作者将攻击形式化为"水印清洗"，提出联合载荷-保真度评估框架（结合比特错误率BER与视觉/语义保持）。对6个OpenAI和Google图像编辑模型、3种代表性水印方案、1800个重建输出进行系统评估，并进行提示消融实验及与传统攻击的对比分析。

Result: 发现两种互补的清洗机制：OpenAI模型在所有评估方案中产生最强的载荷破坏，而Nano Banana 2显示DwtDct在高保真重建下仍脆弱。提示消融表明无需明确的移除指令即可破坏载荷，效应主要由重建路径本身引起。与传统攻击对比显示，提示条件重建构成一种独立的攻击操作界面。

Conclusion: 基础模型重建应被视为不可见水印评估中缺失的鲁棒性条件，现有评估框架需要将此类威胁纳入标准测试。

Abstract: Invisible watermarks are typically evaluated against predefined perturbations such as compression, blur, noise, cropping, and denoising. Public foundation image models expose a distinct threat: an attacker can submit a watermarked image with a single reconstruction prompt and obtain a visually faithful output from which the invisible watermark can no longer be decoded reliably. We formalize this failure mode as watermark laundering and evaluate it using a joint payload-fidelity profile that combines bit error rate (BER) with visual and semantic preservation. Across six OpenAI and Google image editing models, three representative watermarking schemes, and 1,800 reconstructed outputs, we identify two complementary laundering regimes: OpenAI models produce the strongest payload disruption across the evaluated schemes, whereas Nano Banana 2 shows that DwtDct remains vulnerable under high-fidelity reconstruction. Prompt ablations show that no single removal-oriented instruction is necessary for payload disruption, indicating that the effect is primarily induced by the reconstruction pathway rather than by explicit attack wording. Comparisons with conventional attacks further show that prompt-conditioned reconstruction constitutes a distinct operational attack interface. These findings motivate foundation-model reconstruction as a missing robustness condition in invisible watermark evaluation.

</details>


### [88] [MeRoPE: Metric Rotary Position Embedding for Camera-Controlled Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01252)
*Zhijian Qiao,Xinjiang Wang,Jiajie Chen,Haoming Huang,Meng Li,Chih-Chung Chou,Jing Wang,Shaojie Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MeRoPE（Metric Rotary Position Embedding），一种适用于相机控制视频生成的度量旋转位置编码方法，通过正交旋转块编码校准光线间的相对方向、将原始度量位移映射为多频旋转相位，并沿极线弧添加视差锚定对应先验，从而在保持特征范数的同时稳定注意力logits，实现对旋转和翻译的强相机控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的几何感知位置编码（如齐次投影编码）在处理真实世界度量相机轨迹时存在尺度依赖的失败模式：随着物理平移基线增大，注意力logits和特征范数会无界增长，导致生成的相机运动与条件位姿不一致。

Method: 提出MeRoPE编码，包含三个关键设计：（1）用正交旋转块编码校准光线间的相对方向；（2）将原始度量位移映射为多频旋转相位；（3）沿极线弧添加视差锚定的对应先验。该方法严格保持特征范数，使预softmax注意力logits不受物理平移尺度影响，并且对全局刚体坐标变换保持精确不变性。

Result: 在nuScenes（大基线轨迹）和PanShot（多样化相机光学）两个数据集上，MeRoPE相比此前编码方案实现了更强的相机控制，在旋转和翻译两方面均取得生成相机运动与条件位姿之间最佳的一致性。

Conclusion: MeRoPE解决了几何感知位置编码在度量相机轨迹上的尺度崩溃问题，提供了一种范数保持、尺度鲁棒且具备刚体不变性的注意力编码方案，有效提升了视频生成中的相机控制精度。

Abstract: In camera-controlled video generation, geometry-aware positional encodings condition tokens on camera extrinsics and per-token viewing rays. Existing schemes, however, have a scale-dependent failure mode on real-world metric camera trajectories: homogeneous projective encodings cause attention logits and feature norms to grow unbounded with physical translation baselines. We propose MeRoPE (Metric Rotary Position Embedding), a norm-preserving relative camera encoding for attention. MeRoPE encodes relative orientations between calibrated viewing rays with orthogonal rotation blocks, maps raw metric displacements into multi-frequency rotary phases, and adds a disparity-anchored correspondence prior along the epipolar arc. This design strictly preserves feature norms, bounds pre-softmax attention logits regardless of the physical translation scale, and maintains exact invariance to global rigid coordinate changes. Across nuScenes and PanShot, which cover large-baseline trajectories and diverse camera optics, respectively, MeRoPE achieves stronger camera control than prior encodings, with the best consistency between generated camera motion and conditioning poses in both rotation and translation. Code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [89] [Seeing the World and the Self from Egocentric Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01276)
*Kai Guan,Minchao Jiang,Ruichen WangLi,Wentao Zhu,Lei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RESELF框架，首次将第一视角视频中的场景重建与佩戴者全身运动捕捉统一到同一度量坐标系，通过确定性几何回归与扩散式运动生成相结合，在EE4D-JSM数据集上超越了各单任务方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将场景重建和人体运动估计分开处理：场景重建忽略佩戴者，运动估计缺乏显式场景几何且依赖外部轨迹。两个任务存在非对称可见性和不同预测范式（场景可见支持确定性回归，身体严重遮挡需要生成式推断），联合恢复具有挑战性。

Method: RESELF由三部分组成：(1) 将大规模离身（exocentric）数据预训练的几何基础模型适配到第一视角视频，采用帧级尺度和相对位姿一致性目标；(2) 产生的相机轨迹和潜在几何特征条件化一个扩散模型来恢复佩戴者全身运动；(3) 闭环运动学反馈阶段在保持场景几何的同时进一步优化相机轨迹。此外，从EgoExo4D整理了带度量场景几何、相机轨迹和全身运动标注的EE4D-JSM数据集。

Result: 在深度估计、相机跟踪和全身运动估计三个任务上，RESELF均优于针对各单任务设计的SOTA方法。

Conclusion: 通过确定性几何回归与生成式运动推断的耦合设计，可实现在统一度量坐标系下从第一视角视频联合重建场景和还原佩戴者全身运动，为自我中心感知领域提供了新的研究框架和基准数据。

Abstract: Complete 3D perception from egocentric video requires recovering the surrounding scene and the wearer's full-body motion in a shared metric frame. Existing methods typically address scene reconstruction and motion estimation separately: scene reconstruction methods ignore the wearer, whereas motion estimation methods lack explicit scene geometry and often depend on external trajectories. Joint recovery is challenging because the two tasks exhibit asymmetric visibility and require different prediction paradigms. The largely visible scene supports deterministic geometric regression, whereas the severely occluded body requires generative motion inference. We therefore propose RESELF (REconstructing the Scene and the sELF), a unified framework that couples deterministic metric geometry reconstruction with geometry-conditioned motion generation. RESELF adapts a geometry foundation model pre-trained on large-scale exocentric data to egocentric video using frame-wise scale and relative-pose consistency objectives. The resulting camera trajectory and latent geometric features condition a diffusion model that recovers the wearer's motion. A subsequent closed-loop kinematic feedback stage further refines the camera head while preserving the reconstructed scene geometry. To support training and evaluation, we curate EE4D-JSM from EgoExo4D by aligning egocentric video, sparse metric scene geometry, camera trajectories, and full-body motion annotations. Experiments show that RESELF outperforms state-of-the-art methods designed for the individual tasks across depth estimation, camera tracking, and full-body motion estimation. Code, models, and datasets will be available at https://ka1guan.github.io/RESELF/.

</details>


### [90] [TimeSteer: Inference-Time Speech Scheduling in Joint Audio-Visual Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01277)
*Chao Zhou,Yiling Chen,Qi Chu,Tao Gong,Nenghai Yu,Tianyi We*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TimeSteer，一种无需训练的推理时序语音调度框架，通过对预训练联合音视频扩散模型的反向去噪过程进行内容迁移，实现将语音及其对应的视觉发声内容精确放置在用户指定的起止时间间隔内，并引入了首个基准SpeechShift。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练联合音视频扩散模型只能控制"生成什么"（内容），而无法显式控制"何时发生"（时序），尤其是在用户指定的起止时间间隔内安排语音发声是一个尚未解决的任务，且微调主干模型代价高昂。

Method: 本文发现了去噪过程中的两个内在特性：(1) 时序敏感的文本到音频交叉注意力头可以暴露每个话语在潜在时间线上的模型隐含源跨度；(2) 预测的干净潜在表示已组织好了耦合的语音和视觉发声内容，可直接编辑其时间位置而无需重新生成。基于此提出TimeSteer框架，包含Source Span Localization（定位每个话语的源跨度）和Region-Aware Latent Remapping（将源区间的音视频潜在内容迁移到指定目标区间）两个核心模块，全程无需微调。

Result: 在两种代表性骨干网络上实验表明，TimeSteer在区间级时序控制能力上显著优于无训练基线方法，同时保持了具有竞争力的整体生成质量。

Conclusion: 通过利用预训练扩散模型去噪过程中的内在时序敏感性和内容组织特性，可以在不微调的前提下实现精细化的话语级时间间隔控制，为音视频联合生成中的时序编辑提供了新的训练无关思路。

Abstract: Although pretrained joint audio-visual diffusion models offer rich control over \emph{what} to generate, they provide no explicit control over \emph{when} an utterance should occur. To address this, we study \emph{inference-time speech scheduling}, a novel task that places coupled speech and visual articulation within user-specified begin--end intervals without finetuning the backbone model. We uncover two intrinsic properties of the denoising process that enable this task. First, a timing-sensitive text-to-audio cross-attention head exposes each utterance's model-implied source span along the latent timeline. Second, the predicted clean latent already organizes coupled speech and visual articulation, allowing their temporal placement to be edited without regenerating the content. Building on these discoveries, we propose \textbf{TimeSteer}, a training-free framework that localizes each utterance's source span through \textbf{Source Span Localization} and transfers the associated audio-visual latent content from the source interval to the specified target interval through \textbf{Region-Aware Latent Remapping}. We further introduce \textbf{SpeechShift}, the first benchmark for interval-level speech scheduling in joint audio-visual generation. Experiments across two representative backbones show that TimeSteer substantially improves interval controllability over training-free baselines while maintaining competitive overall generation quality.

</details>


### [91] [HiLRP: Toward One Trustworthy Explanation for Vision Transformer: Conservation-Valid Attribution via Attention Primitives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01282)
*Sathiyamohan Nishankar,Pubudu Sanjeewani,Asanka Perera,Selvarajah Thuseethan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HiLRP是一个统一的归因框架，通过将ViT中各种注意力与分辨率降低算子分解为四种操作类型（线性映射、双线性混合、归一化/门控和重索引），每种操作满足守恒的关联规则，从而支持各种ViT架构的归因分析，无需逐架构推导。已证明守恒性和条件等变性，并验证了在14种归因方法和10种架构中，HiLRP是唯一能跨窗口化、空间缩减、多轴和线性注意力模型保持守恒的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Vision Transformer（ViT）架构设计日益多样化（卷积stem、窗口/线性/多轴注意力、patch合并、空间缩减等），而现有归因方法（如Grad-CAM、attention rollout、LRP）的假设往往不适用于各种ViT变体。目前没有任何方法能在这一架构空间中提供统一且可靠的归因框架。

Method: HiLRP将ViT中的注意力与分辨率缩减算子分解为四种基本操作类型：线性映射、双线性混合、归一化/门控和重索引。每种操作都定义了满足守恒性质的关联层相关性传播规则。基于这些规则，新架构可自然支持而非逐架构推导。论文证明了守恒性和条件等变性，并将二者验证到机器精度。

Result: 在涵盖14种归因方法和10种ViT架构的评测中，HiLRP是唯一能在窗口化、空间缩减、多轴和线性注意力模型中保持守恒的方法，而朴素扩展可能产生零或膨胀的关联值。在EfficientViT上的类激活映射归因失败定位任务中，HiLRP达到0.97 Pointing分数，而竞争方法仅为0.55。此外研究发现对空间掩蔽鲁棒的模型上，Faithfulness Correlation指标失效。

Conclusion: HiLRP提供了一种基于架构不可知原则的统一归因框架，通过操作级别的守恒关联规则（而非依赖启发式假设或特定架构推导）实现了跨ViT变体的一致、守恒且可靠的预测归因分解。

Abstract: Vision Transformer (ViT) design has become increasingly diverse, with backbones combining convolutional stems, windowed, linear, or multi-axis attention, patch merging, and spatial reduction in various configurations. This diversity poses challenges for existing attribution methods, whose assumptions often do not hold across ViT variants: Grad-CAM requires a terminal spatial feature map, attention rollout assumes global softmax attention, and layer-wise relevance propagation (LRP) requires module-specific rules. To the best of our knowledge, no existing method provides a unified attribution framework across this architectural space. We show that this architectural diversity can be captured by a simpler underlying structure. The attention and resolution-reduction operators in current ViTs can be decomposed into four operation types: linear maps, bilinear mixing, normalization or gating, and reindexing. Each operation admits a relevance rule that satisfies conservation. Based on these rules, HiLRP supports new backbones by construction rather than by architecture-specific derivation, and its attribution maps decompose the prediction rather than relying on heuristic assumptions. We prove conservation and conditional equivariance and verify both to machine precision. Across 14 attribution methods and 10 architectures, we find that no prior method remains reliable across ViT families, while Faithfulness Correlation becomes uninformative for backbones robust to spatial masking. HiLRP alone preserves conservation across windowed, spatial-reduction, multi-axis, and linear-attention models, where naive extensions can produce zero or inflated relevance. It also localizes attribution failures in class activation mapping, achieving 0.97 Pointing compared with 0.55 for competing methods on EfficientViT.

</details>


### [92] [Agentic Multimodal Models for Environmental Hyperspectral Unmixing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01289)
*Michał Cholewa,Luca Ciampi,Nicola Messina,Przemysław Głomb,Giuseppe Amato*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于大型视觉语言模型（LVLM）的智能体驱动框架，用于优化高光谱解混流水线的输出结果。该框架不替代底层数值算法，而是通过迭代收集光谱与空间证据（光谱库检索、丰度图可视化），对活动端元集执行合并与删除操作并重新估算丰度，从而提升端元数量恢复准确性及光谱签名和丰度图的精度，在多个数据集和多种模块化流水线上均优于或匹敌端到端方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统模块化高光谱解混方法（模型阶数估计→端元提取→丰度估计）逐级传递误差，导致候选成分冗余或歧义，影响最终分解质量。现有方法缺乏对初始分解结果进行交叉验证与修正的机制，因此需要一种既能利用光谱与空间互补证据、又不取代物理可解释数值算法的智能修正框架。

Method: 提出算法无关的LVLM智能体框架：以初始模块化解码为基础，智能体通过专用工具（光谱库检索和丰度图可视化）迭代收集光谱与空间证据，并对活动端元集执行合并（merge）和删除（discard）操作，随后重新估算丰度。将相同的细化流程应用于多种模块化流水线（不同模型阶数估计、端元提取及丰度估计方法的组合）。

Result: 在HYDICE Urban、Jasper Ridge和Stonewall Playa数据集上，智能体一致改善了端元基数（cardinality）精度，并在多数情况下改善了恢复的光谱签名和丰度图；在异构模块化流水线上表现一致，且与集成端到端解混方法（CNN-AE、uDAS、R-CoNMF）相比具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 工具驱动的LVLM智能体能够有效结合光谱与空间证据，对物理可解释的高光谱解混分解进行算法无关的细化修正，展现出在不替代传统数值方法的前提下提升解混质量的重要潜力。

Abstract: Hyperspectral unmixing is a key task in remote sensing that aims to decompose mixed pixels in hyperspectral images into their constituent material signatures, or endmembers, and their fractional abundances. Conventional modular approaches estimate the scene composition through successive model-order estimation, endmember extraction, and abundance estimation stages, whose errors can lead to redundant or ambiguous candidate components and ultimately affect the recovered decomposition. We introduce an algorithm-agnostic, large vision-language model (LVLM)-driven agentic framework that refines the outputs of such pipelines rather than replacing their underlying numerical algorithms. Starting from an initial decomposition, the agent iteratively gathers complementary spectral and spatial evidence through dedicated tools, including spectral-library retrieval and abundance-map visualization, and modifies the active endmember set through merge and discard operations followed by abundance re-estimation. We apply the same refinement procedure to several modular pipelines combining different model-order, extraction, and abundance-estimation methods, and evaluate it on HYDICE Urban, Jasper Ridge, and Stonewall Playa. Experiments show that the proposed agent consistently improves endmember cardinality and generally improves the recovered spectral signatures and abundance maps across heterogeneous modular pipelines, while remaining competitive with integrated end-to-end unmixing methods, including CNN-AE, uDAS, and R-CoNMF. These results highlight the potential of tool-using LVLM agents to combine spectral and spatial evidence for algorithm-agnostic refinement of physically grounded hyperspectral unmixing decompositions. Code is publicly available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/agentic-hu.

</details>


### [93] [CMRVision: A Foundation Model for Cardiac MR Image Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01308)
*Athira J. Jacob,Puneet Sharma,Daniel Rueckert*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CMRVision，一个基于DINOv3风格自监督学习、在多中心多序列3600万张2D CMR图像上预训练的心脏MRI基础模型。通过系统评估架构与训练设计选择，发现领域特定预训练、更小的patch size及patch级目标函数能持续提升下游任务性能。在心血管结构多任务分割和cine视图分类任务中，CMRVision超越自然图像、医学图像、监督学习及其它CMR基础模型基线，并展现出对未见LGE长轴视图的零样本泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心脏磁共振成像（CMR）提供跨多序列、多视角的互补解剖、功能和组织特征信息。目前基础模型主要在自然图像或通用医学图像上预训练，缺乏针对CMR领域特性和多序列/多视角多样性专门设计的预训练模型，导致下游任务性能受限。作者旨在探索针对2D CMR的领域特定基础模型预训练，以提升跨序列、跨视图的心血管分析的鲁棒性和泛化性。

Method: 提出CMRVision基础模型，采用DINOv3风格自监督学习范式，在包含3600万张多中心、多序列2D CMR图像（涵盖cine、LGE、mapping等序列）的队列上进行预训练。作者系统评估了架构选择（如patch size）和训练设计决策（如预训练数据领域、目标函数类型），以确定领域特定预训练的最优配置。预训练后，模型在下游任务上通过迁移学习进行评估，包括多任务分割（cine、LGE、mapping序列的心脏结构分割）和cine视图分类任务。

Result: CMRVision在多项评估中表现最优：多任务分割上与自然图像、医学图像、监督及CMR基础模型基线相比取得最强综合性能，各结构与序列提升一致但幅度温和，Dice分数范围：LV 0.940-0.967、心肌0.855-0.905、RV 0.929、LA 0.920、RA 0.931；最大增益出现在LGE和mapping序列的心肌分割。零样本分割未见的LGE长轴视图取得平均Dice 0.692，证明跨视角泛化能力；cine视图分类取得最高平均准确率0.906。

Conclusion: 领域特定的CMR预训练、更小的patch size和patch级目标函数是提升心脏MRI下游任务性能的关键设计要素。CMRVision展示了跨多序列、多视图的可靠分析和泛化能力，有力支撑了可鲁棒、可泛化心脏MRI基础模型的发展方向，对未来CMR图像分析研究和临床应用的落地具有积极意义。

Abstract: Cardiac magnetic resonance (CMR) imaging provides complementary information on cardiac anatomy, function, and tissue characterization across multiple sequences and views. In this work, we investigate foundation model pretraining for 2D CMR and introduce CMRVision, a CMR-specific foundation model trained using DINOv3-style self-supervised learning on a multi-center, multi-sequence cohort of 36 million CMR images. We systematically evaluate architectural and training design choices for domain-specific pretraining. CMRVision is evaluated on two downstream tasks: multi-task segmentation across cine, late gadolinium enhancement (LGE), and mapping sequences, and cine view classification. Our experiments show that CMR-specific pretraining, smaller patch sizes, and patch-level objectives consistently improve downstream performance. Across a multi-task segmentation benchmark, CMRVision achieved the strongest overall performance, outperforming prior natural-image (NI), medical-image, supervised, and CMR foundation model baselines. Improvements were modest but consistent across structures and sequences, with Dice scores ranging from 0.940-0.967 for LV and 0.855-0.905 for myocardium, and reaching 0.929 for RV, 0.920 for LA, and 0.931 for RA. The largest gains were observed for myocardium segmentation in LGE and mapping images. In a zero-shot segmentation task on unseen LGE long-axis views, the model achieved an average Dice score of 0.692, demonstrating cross-view generalization. For cine view classification, CMRVision achieved the highest average accuracy (0.906), compared to prior methods reported in the literature. These results highlight the potential of CMRVision to support robust and generalizable cardiac MRI analysis across multiple sequences and views.

</details>


### [94] [Reliability Challenges in Diffusion Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01318)
*Md. Atabuzzaman,Chris Thomas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文首次系统性地评估了基于扩散的大视觉语言模型（dLVLMs）在幻觉和偏见方面的可靠性，将其与自回归（AR）LVLMs在四个维度上进行对比，发现dLVLMs在二值视觉查询中反转了AR模型的"是"偏好，存在欠代表性种族群体的准确率崩溃和相反极性的性别偏见，以及在多选题设置中因长度先验导致的准确率崩溃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: dLVLMs作为自回归LVLMs的有力替代方案正在快速发展，具备并行解码、双向上下文和可控生成等优势，但其可靠性属性（幻觉和偏见）尚未得到系统性的刻画和研究，存在明显的研究空白。

Method: 构建了首个系统性的dLVLMs可靠性评估框架，对六个扩散模型和竞争性的自回归基线模型进行基准测试，覆盖四个评估维度，并通过对去噪步骤中token承诺时机和置信度的分析来探究机制性信号。

Result: 研究发现：(1) dLVLMs在二元视觉查询中反转了AR模型的"是"偏好；(2) 幻觉率与AR模型相当但语言质量下降；(3) 在欠代表性种族群体上准确率接近零，并呈现相反极性的性别偏见；(4) 多选题中正确答案比干扰项短时出现准确率崩溃，这与首个去噪步骤即出现的长度先验有关。后期去噪步骤中低置信度的token承诺与幻觉内容相关。

Conclusion: dLVLMs的可靠性模式在不同模型家族间存在差异，表明可靠性受生成范式与训练数据共同塑造；去噪过程中出现的长度先验和低置信度晚期token承诺可作为扩散模型特有的机制性信号，为理解和改进dLVLMs的可靠性提供了新视角。

Abstract: Diffusion-based Large Vision-Language Models (dLVLMs) have recently emerged as a compelling alternative to autoregressive (AR) LVLMs, offering advantages in parallel decoding, bidirectional context, and controllable generation. Despite rapid progress, their reliability properties remain largely uncharacterized. We present the first systematic reliability evaluation of hallucination and bias in dLVLMs, benchmarking six diffusion models against competitive AR baselines across four dimensions. Our key findings are: (1) dLVLMs reverse the yes-bias of AR models in binary visual queries; (2) they achieve competitive hallucination rates yet exhibit degraded linguistic quality; (3) they collapse to near-zero accuracy on underrepresented racial groups with opposite-polarity gender bias; and (4) they exhibit accuracy collapse in multiple-choice settings when the correct option is shorter than its distractors, associated with a length prior that emerges at the first denoising step. Tokens committed at late denoising steps with low confidence further correlate with hallucinated content, pointing to a mechanistic signal unique to diffusion generation. These patterns vary across model families, suggesting reliability is shaped by the generative paradigm together with training data.

</details>


### [95] [ExBind: A Controlled Diagnostic Benchmark for Visual-to-Executable Correspondence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01344)
*Ziqian Wang,Yuxiao Cheng,Tingxiong Xiao,Jinli Suo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ExBind是一个用于诊断多模态编码与编辑系统中"视觉到可执行对象"对应层（binding）的受控基准。它通过从语义定位与动作执行中隔离出该层，构建确定性映射到可执行引用的SVG、DOM、canvas、树、图、表格等测试用例，并仅要求模型输出严格引用。基准包含250例广泛套件、240例定向套件和50个配对潜在组。结果显示Qwen2.5-VL-3B候选有效性98.4%但精确准确率仅76.4%，Qwen3-VL-4B有效性与精确准确率分别达100.0%和98.8%。在定向表格套件中，其余错误均为"行对列错"的选择。该基准用于受控诊断而非大规模排名或端到端评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态编码与编辑系统需将可见或语义指代物映射到可精确编辑的可执行对象上，但错误的引用可能选中一个有效却不正确的节点（如DOM节点、SVG元素、图端点、层级成员或表格单元）。由于执行本身成功掩盖了错误来源，现有方法无法定位故障发生在视觉定位层还是动作执行层。ExBind旨在将视觉到可执行对象的对应层隔离出来，作为受控诊断基准进行精确评估。

Method: ExBind采样与表示无关的潜在绑定（latent binding）实例，并将其编译为SVG、DOM、canvas、树、图、表格六类用例，具有到可执行引用的确定性映射。模型仅需输出严格的引用，评估器将预测映射回潜在结构并依据结构约束打分，无需推理轨迹。基准包含250例broad套件、240例disjoint targeted套件及50个配对潜在组；同时通过候选顺序扰动测试候选顺序对结果的影响。

Result: Qwen2.5-VL-3B在broad套件上候选有效性达98.4%，但精确准确率仅76.4%；Qwen3-VL-4B有效性与精确准确率分别为100.0%和98.8%。在定向表格套件中，Qwen2.5-VL-3B的残余错误全部表现为"正确行/错误列"的选择。候选顺序扰动会改变用例级结果，但错误模式保持稳定。Code与benchmark数据均公开提供。

Conclusion: ExBind定位为受控诊断工具而非大规模模型排名或端到端编辑评估基准。它揭示了即使在候选对象有效（结构合法）的情况下，模型仍可能出现引用不精确的问题（如行对而列错），且不同规模/代际模型在binding能力上存在显著差距。恢复准确的潜在结构对应是构建可靠多模态编辑系统的关键前提。

Abstract: Multimodal coding and editing systems must map a visible or semantic referent to the exact executable object that can be edited. A wrong reference may select a valid but incorrect DOM node, SVG element, graph endpoint, hierarchy member, or table cell, while final execution success alone does not reveal the source of the failure. ExBind isolates this visual-to-executable correspondence layer as a controlled diagnostic benchmark between semantic localization and action execution. It samples representation-independent latent binding instances and compiles them into SVG, DOM, canvas, tree, graph, and table cases with deterministic mappings to executable references. Models output only a strict reference; the evaluator maps predictions back to latent structure and scores structural constraints without requiring reasoning traces. The release contains a 250-case broad suite, a disjoint 240-case targeted suite, and 50 paired latent groups. Qwen2.5-VL-3B achieves 98.4% candidate validity but 76.4% exact accuracy, while Qwen3-VL-4B achieves 100.0% validity and 98.8% exact accuracy. In the targeted table suite, all Qwen2.5-VL-3B residual errors are valid correct-row/wrong-column selections. Candidate-order perturbations change case-level outcomes while preserving this error pattern. ExBind is designed for controlled diagnosis rather than population-scale ranking or end-to-end editing evaluation. Code and benchmark records are available at https://github.com/Daerwang2020/Exbind and https://huggingface.co/datasets/Ziqianwwww/ExBind.

</details>


### [96] [Accurate Reconstruction of Gas Turbine Blade Geometry Using 3D/2D Rigid Registration and CT View Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01368)
*Hristo Valtchanov,Nicolas Piché,Vladimir Brailovski,Justin Byers,Catherine Désrosiers,François Guibault*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种多部分3D-2D刚性配准方法，将CAD模型直接与X射线投影图像对齐，替代CT重建用于燃气轮机叶片等复杂内部结构零件的无损检测和尺寸测量。该方法采用贪婪配准算法逐步对齐叶片外部和内部组件，并通过贪婪视角优化方法选择最佳拍摄角度，实现了亚像素级配准精度（误差低于放大探测器像素间距的五分之一）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 燃气轮机叶片等制造件的内部冷却通道复杂结构直接影响热性能和使用寿命，需要非破坏性X射线和CT检测来确保尺寸精度。传统的CT重建方法用于零件检测和测量成本高、效率低，因此需要探索直接基于X射线投影进行配准和测量的替代方案，以降低复杂度并提高检测效率。

Method: 提出一种多部分3D-2D刚性配准方法：(1) 采用贪婪配准算法，先对齐叶片外部几何，再依次配准内部组件，通过最大化仿真X射线图像与采集图像之间的互信息来实现对齐；(2) 分步式配准降低了问题复杂度并提高对齐精度；(3) 采用贪婪方法迭代优化视角选择，以最小化尺寸测量误差。

Result: 结果表明少量斜视角度可获得最佳精度，但较宽的角度范围也能产生可接受的结果。该方法达到亚像素配准精度，误差低于放大探测器像素间距的五分之一。图像噪声和缺陷会降低配准精度，但直接在投影空间中进行配准相比CT重建能减轻这些不利影响。适当的视角选择可在存在图像噪声和缺陷的情况下保持可接受的亚像素精度。

Conclusion: 该3D-2D投影空间直接配准方法可作为CT重建的可行替代方案用于零件检测和测量，通过合理的视角优化策略，即使在噪声和缺陷干扰下也能保持亚像素级精度，为复杂内部结构零件的工业检测提供了一条更高效的路径。

Abstract: Non-destructive X-ray and computed tomography (CT) testing are essential for ensuring the dimensional accuracy of manufactured components with complex internal structures, such as the cooling channels in gas turbine blades, which directly affect thermal performance and service life. This study presents a multipart 3D-2D rigid registration approach for aligning CAD models with X-ray projections as an alternative to CT reconstruction for part inspection and measurement. A greedy registration algorithm sequentially aligns the blade's exterior before registering its internal components by maximizing the mutual information between simulated and acquired X-ray images. This stepwise approach reduces problem complexity and improves alignment accuracy. View angles are optimized using a greedy method that iteratively selects angles to minimize dimensional measurement errors. The results indicate that a small number of oblique views provides the best accuracy, although a broad range of angles yields acceptable results. The method achieves subpixel registration accuracy, with errors below one-fifth of the magnified detector-pixel pitch. Image noise and defects reduce registration precision, but direct registration in projection space mitigates these effects compared with CT reconstruction. Appropriate view selection can therefore preserve acceptable subpixel accuracy in the presence of image noise and defects.

</details>


### [97] [Diffusion Based Unpaired Data Learning for Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01370)
*Chenglong Bao,Yiming Dang,Chenguang Duan,Yuling Jiao,Defeng Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LUD-DIF是一种基于扩散模型的逆问题求解方法，旨在利用无配对数据解决逆问题中配对数据稀缺的困境。该方法从联合分布的证据下界（ELBO）出发，在弱耦合假设下将其解耦为两个独立的扩散过程，并从变分推断视角提供理论支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多基于深度学习的逆问题求解器依赖大量配对数据，但在实际场景中获取足够的配对数据极具挑战性，而无配对数据获取成本低廉。为了最大化数据利用率，需要一种能够利用无配对数据求解逆问题的方法。

Method: 提出LUD-DIF框架，从联合分布的ELBO出发，在弱耦合假设下将其解耦为两个独立的扩散过程，从而允许多个扩散模型独立训练。该方法从变分推断视角推导损失函数，定量分析弱耦合假设引入的误差上界，并给出基于定理的启发式超参数选择策略。

Result: 在多个图像逆问题上的实验结果表明，LUD-DIF取得了优异的性能，验证了其在无配对逆问题设置下的有效性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: LUD-DIF通过解耦联合分布的ELBO为两个独立扩散过程，成功实现了无配对数据的逆问题求解，并提供了从理论推导到误差分析再到超参数选择的完整方法论支撑。

Abstract: Data is important in many deep learning-based inverse problem solvers. However, obtaining sufficient paired data in many scenarios remains highly challenging, while unpaired data is cheap. To maximize data utilization, this paper proposes LUD-DIF, a diffusion-based approach for solving inverse problems with unpaired data. Starting from the evidence lower bound (ELBO) of the joint distribution, we decouple it into two independent diffusion processes under the weak-coupling assumption. The method provides theoretical support from a variational inference perspective, derives the loss function, quantitatively analyzes the error bound introduced by the assumption, and offers a theorem-motivated heuristic for hyperparameter selection. Experimental results demonstrate that LUD-DIF achieves outstanding performance on multiple image inverse problems, validating its effectiveness and generalization capability in unpaired inverse problem settings.

</details>


### [98] [IntroConformal: Conformal Factuality Guarantees for Large Vision-Language Models via Introspective Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01375)
*Md. Atabuzzaman,Christian Alexander,Chris Thomas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出IntroConformal，一种基于模型自身内省信号的无需训练的共形风险控制（CRC）框架，为大型视觉语言模型（LVLM）提供有限样本、分布无关的事实性保证，无需外部验证器或生成时置信度信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法确保LVLM生成内容事实性时，通常依赖外部验证器或生成时的置信度信号，但置信度信号对"自信但错误"的输出常失效，外部验证器则引入辅助依赖。作者认为可以通过模型自身的内省信号实现可靠的事实性控制。

Method: 提出IntroConformal，一种无需训练的共形风险控制（CRC）框架。通过两类模型内省一致性分数实现：(1) 层间语义稳定性（layer-wise semantic stability），从隐藏状态表示导出的符合性分数；(2) 验证概率（verification probability），捕捉模型对声明事实性的自我管理判断的更强分数。

Result: 在多个LVLM架构上，IntroConformal满足共形风险保证，同时显著减少弃权（abstention），并在声明级判别能力上与基于外部验证器的方法相比达到竞争性或更优性能。

Conclusion: 证明了仅通过模型内省信号即可在无需训练和外部依赖的条件下实现可靠的事实性统计保证，为LVLM事实性控制提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) have achieved strong multimodal performance, yet ensuring the factual correctness of generated content remains challenging. Existing methods that provide statistical guarantees on factuality typically rely on external verifiers or generation-time confidence signals, which introduce auxiliary dependencies or often fail for confident but incorrect outputs. We argue that reliable factuality control can instead be achieved through introspective signals derived from the model itself. We introduce IntroConformal, a training-free Conformal Risk Control (CRC) framework that provides finite-sample, distribution-free factuality guarantees. We first instantiate it with layer-wise semantic stability, a conformity score derived from hidden-state representations, and then propose verification probability, a stronger score capturing the model's self-administered judgment on claim factuality. Across multiple LVLM architectures, IntroConformal satisfies the conformal risk guarantee while substantially reducing abstention and achieving competitive or superior claim-level discrimination relative to external verifier-based baselines.

</details>


### [99] [Multimodal RGB-Infrared Combination for UAV-Based Wildfire Segmentation: A Comparative Study on FLAME3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01390)
*Matheus F. Kovaleski,Luís Garrote,Cristiano Premebida,Jérôme Mendes,João Ruivo Paulo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了UAV(无人机)野火监测中RGB与红外图像的融合策略,在FLAME3数据集上对比了三种图像分割架构(U-Net、DeepLabV3+、SegFormer)下的RGB/红外基线与三种代表性融合策略,发现热红外信息在UAV分割中起主导作用,基于Transformer的架构配合特征级多模态融合是最有前景的方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UAV因其灵活性、低成本及获取高分辨率图像的能力,成为消防作业的重要平台。虽然深度学习已显著提升UAV野火监测能力,但现有研究对RGB与红外模态各自的贡献程度、融合时机的影响以及不同分割架构如何利用多模态信息尚缺乏系统分析。本文旨在填补这一空白,为野火分割的多模态融合提供系统性评估。

Method: 在FLAME3数据集上进行RGB-红外二值野火分割实验。具体包括:1)构建RGB和红外单模态基线;2)对比三种代表性融合策略(输入级/特征级/决策级);3)在三种分割架构(U-Net、DeepLabV3+、SegFormer)上系统评估这些策略。从模态贡献度、融合时机和架构对多模态信息的利用方式三个维度进行分析。

Result: 实验发现热红外(Thermal)信息在UAV野火分割中扮演主导角色;基于Transformer的架构(SegFormer)在利用多模态信息方面表现更优;特征级的融合策略与Transformer架构结合取得了最佳效果。

Conclusion: 研究揭示红外模态在UAV野火分割中的核心地位,并指出特征级多模态融合配合Transformer架构是未来研究的最有前景方向,为后续野火监测系统的多传感器融合设计提供了重要指导。

Abstract: Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) have emerged as a promising platform for firefighting operations due to their flexibility, low operational cost, and ability to acquire high-resolution imagery in locations that may be difficult or dangerous to access using conventional methods. Recent advances in deep learning have significantly improved the capabilities of UAV-based wildfire monitoring systems. The present work investigates RGB-infrared fusion for binary wildfire segmentation on the FLAME3 dataset. In this Study, RGB and Infrared baselines are compared with three representative fusion strategies across three segmentation architectures, including U-Net, DeepLabV3+, and SegFormer. The key motivation of this work is to analyze the contribution of each modality, evaluate the impact of fusion timing, and examine how different network architectures exploit multimodal information for UAV wildfire delineation. The findings indicate that thermal information plays a dominant role in UAV segmentation and that feature-level multimodal fusion combined with transformer-based architectures offers the most promising direction for future research.

</details>


### [100] [Scale-based Approach for Active Wildfire Segmentation on Satellite Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01392)
*Matheus F. Kovaleski,Cristiano Premebida,João Ruivo Paulo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了利用多光谱Landsat-8影像进行主动火灾分割的方法，提出了一种基于连通组件分析和四分位距准则的数据驱动协议来刻画火灾区域尺寸分布，并评估了U-Net、DeepLabV3+和SegFormer三种架构在不同SWIR光谱配置下的鲁棒性。结果发现U-Net鲁棒性最强，SegFormer表现接近，DeepLabV3+预测偏保守；SWIR2波段在各架构中表现最佳或接近最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卫星遥感主动火灾制图面临火灾像素稀疏且高度不平衡的挑战，尤其在早期或低密度火灾观测中更为突出。现有研究缺乏对不同尺度火灾区域条件下模型鲁棒性的系统性评估，且尚未充分探索光谱波段选择与架构设计对主动火灾分割性能的联合影响。
